# API functionality matrix

1453 explicitly exported method/endpoint pairs in 899 canonical route files. Implicit framework HEAD/OPTIONS behavior requires runtime testing; not silently counted as explicit implementations. File-level auth/query signals do NOT prove method-level enforcement. Static source coverage is complete for enumerated files, functional execution coverage is not. Actual production response remains unobserved except bounded probes in runtime audit. NOT VERIFIED means not fully executed/reviewed; PARTIAL means named source-path repairs have isolated tests but not end-to-end verification; DISABLED is source quarantine, not deployed artifact proof. Known unresolved source defects are listed separately in FUNCTIONALITY_REPAIR_LOG.md; these are not concealed by the general default.
